     range.

     CORPORATE GOVERNANCE PRACTICES OF THE COMPANY                             本公司的企業管治常規

     The Group is committed to achieving high standards of corporate           本集團致力達致高水準的企業管治，務
     governance to safeguard the interests of the Shareholders and to          求保障股東權益及提高企業價值及問責
     enhance corporate value and accountability.                               性。

     The Company has applied the principles as set out in the Corporate        本公司已應用上市規則附錄十四所載企
     Governance Code and also the corporate governance report as               業管治守則的原則及企業管治報告。
     contained in Appendix 14 to the Listing Rules.

     The Corporate Governance Code sets out the principles of good             企業管治守則訂明的良好企業管治原則
     corporate governance and two levels of corporate governance               及兩個層面的企業管治常規如下：
     practices, as follows:

     (a)   code provisions, which listed issuers are expected to comply with   (a)   守則條文，期望上市發行人遵守或
           or to give considered reasons for deviation; and                          就偏離行為提供審慎考慮的理由；
                                                                                     及

     (b)   recommended best practices for guidance only, which listed          (b)   建議最佳常規，僅屬指引，鼓勵上
           issuers are encouraged to comply with.                                    市發行人遵守。

     The Company ’ s corporate governance practices are based on               本公司的企業管治常規乃以企業管治守
     the principles and the code provisions as set out in the Corporate        則所載原則及守則條文為依據。
     Governance Code.

     provision A.2.1, the Company regularly reviews its compliance with        本公司定期審閱遵守企業管制守則的情
     corporate governance codes and the Company was in compliance with         況且本公司於截至 2018 年 12 月 31 日止

A.   THE BOARD                                                                A.   董事會

     1.   Responsibilities                                                         1.   責任

          The Board is responsible for the leadership and control of                    董事會負責領導和控制本公司
          the Company and is responsible for promoting the success                      及負責統籌及監督本公司的事
          of the Company by directing and supervising its affairs. The                  務進而促使本公司達致成功。
          Board also reviews the policies and practices on compliance                   董事會亦審閱遵守法律及法規
          with legal and regulatory requirements. The Board has                         規定方面的政策及常規。董事
          established Board committees and has delegated to these                       會下設董事委員會，並向該等
          Board committees’ various responsibilities as set out in their                董事委員會轉授其各自職權範
          respective terms of reference which are published on the                      圍載列的各項責任。該等董事
          websites of the Stock Exchange and the Company.                               會委員會各自的職權範圍刊載
                                                                                        於聯交所及本公司網站。

          All of the Directors have carried out their duties in good faith              全體董事已真誠地依據適用法
          and in compliance with the standards of applicable laws                       律及規例的標準履行職責，並
          and regulations, and have acted in the best interests of the                  時刻為本公司及其股東的最佳
          Company and its Shareholders at all times.                                    利益行事。

                                                                                        項。

          Company’s expense.                                                            見，有關開支由本公司承擔。

B.   CHAIRMAN AND CHIEF EXECUTIVE                                           B.   主席及行政總裁

     Code provision A.2.1 of the Corporate Governance Code                       企業管治守則守則條文 A.2.1 條訂
     stipulates that the roles of chairman and chief executive should be         明，主席及行政總裁的職位應予區
     separate and should not be performed by the same individual.                分，並不應由同一人兼任。

     With effect from 9 December 2018, Ms. Dang Chunxiang resigned               自 2018 年 12 月 9 日 起， 黨 春 香 女
     as chief executive officer of the Company and Mr. Shi Yonghong,             士辭任本公司行首席執行官，現任
     the existing chairman was appointed as the chief executive officer          主席施永宏先生獲委任為本公司首
     of the Company. As such, the roles of the chairman and the chief            席執行官。因此，本公司主席及首
     executive officer of the Company are performed by the same                  席執行官的職位由同一人兼任，並
     individual and constitute a deviation from Code provision A.2.1 of          構成偏離企業管治守則守則條文
     the Corporate Governance Code.                                              A.2.1 條。

     Mr. Shi Yonghong had assumed both the roles of the chairman                 由於施永宏先生在海底撈集團的食
     and the chief executive officer of the Company as he has                    品和餐飲服務行業以及管理經驗方
     extensive experience and knowledge in the food and the catering             面擁有豐富的經驗和知識，及為提
     service industry and management experience within the Haidilao              高本公司營運效率，故彼兼任本公
     Group and in order to improve the efficiency of the operations of           司主席及首席執行官。
     the Company.

     In view of the ever-changing business environment in which the              鑒於本集團經營的業務環境不斷變
     Group operates, the chairman and the chief executive officer must           化，主席及首席執行官必須精通中
     be proficient in the Chinese condiment market and be sensitive              國 調 味 品 市 場， 並 對 市 場 變 化 敏
     to market changes in order to promote the businesses of the                 感，以促進本集團的業務。因此，
     Group. The Board thus considers a segregation of the role of the            董事會認為，主席及首席執行官的
     chairman and chief executive officer may create unnecessary                 角色分離可能會為本集團的日常營
     costs for the daily operations of the Group.                                運帶來不必要的成本。

     The Board considers that vesting two roles in Mr. Shi Yonghong              董事會認為，施永宏先生擔任兩個
     enables the Company to promptly and efficiently make and                    職位，可使本公司迅速有效地制定
     implement decisions and will not impair the balance of power                及實施決策，且不會損害董事會與
     and authority between the Board and the management of the                   本公司管理層之間的權力及權限平
     Company. The Company has established board committees,                      衡。本公司已成立董事委員會，即
     namely, the Audit Committee, the Remuneration Committee,                    審計委員會、薪酬委員會及提名委
     and the Nomination Committee, with their members mainly                     員會，其成員主要由獨立非執行董
     comprising of independent non-executive Directors and which                 事組成，並負責重要的企業管治職
     are responsible for the important corporate governance functions.           能。擁有適合本公司業務的技能及
     The three independent non-executive Directors who possess                   經驗平衡的三名獨立非執行董事亦
     balance of skills and experience appropriate to the business of             為董事會提供寶貴的獨立意見。所
     the Company also contribute valuable independent views to the               有重大決策都是在與董事會成員和
     Board. All major decisions have been made in consultation with              相關委員會以及高級管理團隊協商
     members of the Board and appropriate committees, as well as the             後作出。因此，董事會認為，已有
     senior management team. The Board is therefore of the view that             適 當 的 權 力 平 衡 和 保 障 措 施。 然
     there are adequate balance of power and safeguards in place.                而，董事會將繼續定期監察及檢討
     Nevertheless, the Board will continue to regularly monitor and              本公司目前的架構，並於適當時候
     review the Company’s current structure and to make necessary                作出必要的變更。
     changes at an appropriate time.

     H.    RISK MANAGEMENT AND INTERNAL CONTROLS                                    H.   風險管理及內部控制

           The Board of Directors is responsible for the establishment                   公司董事會負責建立和維持與本集
           and maintenance of sound and effective risk management and                    團戰略目標相匹配的、健全有效的
           internal control systems that are in line with the strategic objective        風險管理及內部監控系統。本集團
           of the Group. The Group established risk management and                       建立風險管理及內部監控系統，旨
           internal control systems to protect the interests of the Group and            在保障本集團和股東利益，確保本
           shareholders, ensure the Group is in compliance with relevant                 集團遵守相關法律法規，有效識別
           laws and regulations, effectively identify and manage significant             和管理影響公司戰略目標實現的重
           risks in achieving its strategic objectives, protect the safety               大風險，保護本集團資產的安全，
           of the Group’s assets, and ensure the maintenance of proper                   保證恰當合規的會計記錄和財務報
           compliance accounting records and financial reports.                          告。

           The Board of Directors is responsible for evaluating the nature               公司董事會負責評估本集團達成戰
           and extent of the risks the Group is willing to take in achieving             略目標時所願意接納的風險性質及
           strategic objectives and ensuring that the Group establishes                  程度，並確保本集團設立及維持合
           and maintains appropriate and effective risk management and                   適及有效的風險管理及內部監控系
           internal control systems as well as overseeing management of the              統，同時，負責監督本集團管理層
           Group in the design, implementation and monitoring of the risk                對風險管理及內部監控系統的設
           management and internal control systems.                                      計、實施與監察。

           The Board periodically receives (at least once a year) reports from           公司董事會定期（至少每年一次）收
           the Group’s management regarding financial, operational and                   到本集團管理層關於本集團的財

           assessment of the internal control and risk management functions              控及風險管理建立、審閱及評估的
           of the Group. All significant risks are reported to the Board. The            報告。所有重大的風險均會向董事

           plan. The Group would review, among other things, adequacy of                 對計劃做出評估。本集團會審核其

           and budget of our accounting, internal control and financial                  方面的資源、員工資歷及經驗是否
           reporting functions.                                                          足夠，以及培訓課程及有關預算是
                                                                                         否充足。

2.   Distributors Performance and Inventory Management                  2.   經銷商表現及存貨管理

     We closely monitor the performance of our distributors. For             我們密切監察經銷商的表現。
     example, our sales personnel conduct inspections of our                 例如，我們的銷售人員不時檢
     distributors from time to time to monitor their sales, sales            查經銷商以監察其銷售、銷售
     prices, marketing activities, storage conditions, logistics             價、 市 場 營 銷 活 動、 儲 存 條
     facilities, staff quality, quality control and inventory levels.        件、物流設施、員工素質、質
     Generally, we require our distributors to report to us their            量 控 制 及 存 貨 水 平。 一 般 而
     inventory records every two weeks to cross-check with our               言，我們要求經銷商每兩個星
     internal data base. Our staff also conduct monthly inventory            期向我們匯報其存貨記錄，以
     checks at randomly selected distributors. When we notice                與我們的內部數據庫進行交叉
     that our distributors have excessive inventories or their               核對。我們的員工亦每月核查
     sales volumes drop significantly, we may make inquiries and             隨機抽選經銷商的存貨情況。

           Risk of Quality and Safety                                                   質量與安全風險

           Food safety and quality control are the top priority in the                  食品安全和質量控制是食品加工企
           production and operation for food processing enterprises. If our             業生產經營的重中之重，一旦我們
           quality control system is not sufficiently implemented, quality or           的質量控制體系出現控制不到位，
           food safety issues may arise, which will bring a material adverse            則有可能出現質量或食品安全問
           impact on the company’s brand, reputation and financial position.            題，從而對公司的品牌、聲譽及財
                                                                                        務狀況造成重大不利影響。

           Response measures: The Group has passed the Hazard Analysis                  應 對 措 施： 本 集 團 已 經 通 過 危 害
           Critical Control Point (HACCP) System Certification and the ISO9001          分 析 重 要 控 制 點 (HACCP) 體 系 及
           Quality Assurance System Certification. From the process of raw              ISO9001 質量保證體系認證，自原
           materials import to products delivery, strict implementation has             料引入至產品出廠銷售均嚴格按照
           been made in accordance with the requirements of such systems                體系要求執行，以保障食品的安全
           to ensure food safety and product quality. At the same time,                 及確保產品的品質。同時，本集團
           according to our “Food Safety Risk Assessment Criteria”, the Group           根據我們的    《食品安全風險評估標
           will conduct comprehensive risk assessments for food safety and              準》，從法規風險、產品風險、運營
           quality under three major aspects: regulatory risk, product risk and         風險三個大的方面對食品安全與質
           operational risk. The Group will identify risk points through such           量進行全方位的風險評估，通過風
           risk assessments and track the effectiveness of risk rectification to        險評估識別風險點，並跟蹤評估風
           ensure that risk control of the Group’s operation is in place.               險整改的效果，以確保本集團運營
                                                                                        的風險控制到位。

    Risk of Connected Transactions                                            關聯交易風險

    As connected transactions account for a relatively high proportion        由於關聯交易在本集團收入中佔比
    of the Group’s revenue, on one hand, the sales revenue of the             較高，一方面如果關聯方盈利能力
    Group may be negatively affected if the profitability of related          大幅下降或品牌受損，可能會對本
    parties is substantially declined or the brand is damaged; on the         集團的銷售收入產生負面影響；另
    other hand, the Group shall comply with the pricing principles and        一方面，本集團需遵守與關聯方交
    approved annual caps of the transactions with related parties,            易的定價原則及已獲批准的年度上
    otherwise the Group will be exposed to non-compliance risks in            限，否則本集團的公司治理將面臨
    corporate governance.                                                     不合規的風險。

    Response measures: At present, the Group has established a                應對措施：本集團目前已設有較為
    comprehensive internal control system to ensure that the terms            全面的內部監控系統，以確保關連
    of connected transactions are fair and reasonable, and the actual         交易的條款屬公平合理，以及關連
    amount incurred from connected transactions is controlled                 交易的實際產生金額控制在相關年
    within the relevant annual caps. Meanwhile, the Group will also           度上限之內；本集團同時也將繼續
    upgrade and develop diversified product portfolio by continuously         通過拓展並深化銷售渠道，升級並
    expanding and deepening its sales channels, and further                   開發多元化產品組合，進一步提升
    improving third-party sales revenue in order to reduce the Group’s        第三方銷售收入，藉以減少本集團
    reliance on related parties.                                              對關聯方的依賴。

ABOUT THE REPORT                                                           關於本報告

Yihai International Holding Limited (hereinafter “Yihai”, “the Group” or    頤海國際控股有限集團 （下 稱「頤 海 」、
“we”) is pleased to present our report on Environmental, Social and        「本集團」或「我們」）欣然呈列 2018 年度
Governance (ESG) for the year 2018 (“the Report”). This is the third        之環境、社會及管治報告（「本報告」）。
consecutive ESG report we have disclosed to the public regarding            本報告系我們連續第三年向社會發佈本
our ESG management philosophy and performance of our practices.             集團在環境、社會及管治（「ESG」）方面
We hope that the Report will disclose the concepts and practices on         的管理理念與實踐績效表現，我們希望
sustainable development of the Group in a transparent manner and            本報告披露能以具透明度之方式傳遞本
enhance the mutual understanding and communication of the various           集團的可持續發展理念與實踐，促進各
stakeholders of the Group.                                                  利益相關方對本集團的了解和溝通。

      Environmental, Social and Governance Concept                                環境、社會及管治理念

      We believe that sustainable and robust ESG performance is essential         我們相信持續穩健的 ESG 表現對本集團
      to the sustainable development of the Group ’ s business and the            業務與本集團所在社區的可持續發展至
      community where the Group operates. The dynamic combination of              關重要。商業和社會需求的有機結合方
      commercial and social needs is bound to embrace more influential            能產生更具影響的社會效應，我們不僅
      social effects. We are not only committed to achieving sound financial      致力於實現強健的財務業績，亦致力於
      performance, but also realising greater overall benefits through actively   積極識別各利益相關方的期望，不斷發
      identifying stakeholders ’ expectations, discovering areas where            掘自身業務發展和環境、社會需求的契
      our business development coheres with environmental and social              合點，審慎管理自身的 ESG 風險，充分
      demands, exercising prudent management over our ESG risks, and              整合現有及潛在資源以為社會創造更具
      fully integrating our existing and potential resources to provide more      價值的產品和服務，實現更大的整體效
      value-added products and services for the society.                          益。

      Environmental, Social and Governance Management System                      環境、社會及管治管理體系

      In order to effectively incorporate ESG management concept into the         為 了 更 有 效 地 將 ESG 管 理 理 念 融 入 本
      Group’s strategic decision-making and guide the implementation of           集團戰略決策，進一步指導相關工作落
      relevant tasks, the Group has set up a management system under              實，本集團建立了由董事會負責 ESG 戰
      which the Board of Directors is in charge of the development of ESG         略和目標制定，管理層負責 ESG 戰略和
      strategy and objective, the senior management is in charge of the           目標的具體實施，本集團總部多個部門
      implementation of such strategy and objective, and the ESG working          組 成 的 環 境、 社 會 及 管 治 (ESG) 工 作 小
      team consisting of a number of departments in the headquarters of the       組，在管理層的指導下，開展各項 ESG
      Group carries out various ESG tasks under the guidance of the senior        工 作 的 管 理 體 系。ESG 管 理 體 系 的 建
      management. This management system secures the performance of               立，為本集團履行 ESG 責任提供了堅實
      ESG responsibilities for the Group.                                         的保障。

reducing the carbon footprint; the Group also reduces negative impact            燃油及尾氣排放，減少運輸對所到環境

Remuneration and Welfare Benefits                                             薪酬福利與待遇

As an equal opportunity employer, the Group abides by the national            作為平等機會的僱主，本集團遵守國家
laws and regulations regarding minimum wage and the basic welfare.            關於各地最低工資標準和基本福利制度
Employees are entitled to various social benefits and public holidays         的法律法規要求，員工依法享有各類法
according to relevant laws. In order to strengthen the capacity of our        定福利和法定假期。為了強化人才隊伍
talent team, the Group improves the fair and transparent remuneration         建設，本集團完善了公平透明的薪酬考
review and promotion mechanism, providing competitive remuneration            核與晉升機制，根據業務和崗位要求，

      products in-stock, the defective products would be isolated in a timely    題，第一時間將問題產品進行隔離，避

doing so would reasonably be expected to outweigh the public interest     生的公眾利益，我們決定不應在報告中
benefits of such communication.                                           溝通該事項。

                         IFRS 16 Leases                                                            國際財務報告準則第 16
                                                                                                   號租賃

                         Nature of change                                                          變動性質

                         IFRS 16 was issued in January 2016. It will result in                     國際財務報告準則第 16
                         almost all leases being recognised on the balance                         號 於 2016 年 1 月 頒 佈，
                         sheet by lessees, as the distinction between operating                    由於經營租賃與融資租

            2.3 Principles of consolidation and equity accounting                         2.3 合併及權益會計原則

                  (i)    Subsidiaries                                                       (i)   附屬公司

                         Subsidiaries are all entities (including structured                      附屬公司指本集團擁有
                         entities) over which the Group has control. The Group                    控制權的所有實體（包括
                         controls an entity when the Group is exposed to, or                      結構性實體）。當本集團
                         has rights to, variable returns from its involvement with                透過參與實體業務而享
                         the entity and has the ability to affect those returns                   有或有權取得該實體的
                         through its power to direct the activities of the entity.                可變回報且有能力透過
                         Subsidiaries are fully consolidated from the date on                     其指揮該實體業務的權
                         which control is transferred to the Group. They are                      力影響該等回報時，則
                         deconsolidated from the date that control ceases.                        本集團控制該實體。附
                                                                                                  屬公司自控制權轉移至
                                                                                                  本集團之日起全部合併
                                                                                                  入賬，並自控制終止之
                                                                                                  日起取消合併。

                         The acquisition method of accounting is used to                          本集團採用收購會計法
                         account for business combinations by the Group (refer                    將業務合併入賬（請參閱
                         to Note 2.4).                                                            附註 2.4）。

                         Intercompany transactions, balances and unrealised                       集團公司間的公司間交
                         gains on transactions between group companies are                        易、結餘及未變現交易
                         eliminated. Unrealised losses are also eliminated unless                 收益予以對銷。未變現
                         the transaction provides evidence of an impairment                       虧損亦會予以抵銷，除
                         of the transferred asset. Accounting policies of                         非有關交易顯示所轉讓
                         subsidiaries have been changed where necessary to                        資產的減值證據。附屬
                         ensure consistency with the policies adopted by the                      公司的會計政策已按需
                         Group.                                                                   要調整，以確保與本集
                                                                                                  團採納的政策貫徹一致。

                         Non-controlling interests in the results and equity of                   業績內的非控股權益及

     2.4 Business combinations                                                  2.4 業務合併

          The acquisition method of accounting is used to account                 本集團採用收購會計法將所有
          for all business combinations, regardless of whether equity             業務合併入賬，而不論收購股

            2.11 Impairment of non-financial assets                                     2.11 非金融資產減值

                  Intangible assets that have an indefinite useful life are                可使用年期不限定的無形資產
                  not subject to amortisation and are tested annually for                  毋須攤銷，但每年須就減值進
                  impairment, or more frequently if events or changes in                   行測試，或於發生事項或情況
                  circumstances indicate that they might be impaired. Other                出現變化而顯示可能出現減值
                  assets are tested for impairment whenever events or                      時作出更頻密的減值測試。其
                  changes in circumstances indicate that the carrying amount               他資產會於發生事項或情況出
                  may not be recoverable. An impairment loss is recognised                 現變化而顯示其賬面值未必能
                  for the amount by which the asset ’ s carrying amount                    夠收回時，進行減值測試。減
                  exceeds its recoverable amount. The recoverable amount                   值虧損按有關資產賬面值超逾
                  is the higher of an asset’s fair value less costs of disposal            其可收回金額的數額確認。可
                  and value in use. For the purposes of assessing impairment,              收回金額為資產的公平值減出
                  assets are grouped at the lowest levels for which there                  售成本與使用價值兩者的較高
                  are separately identifiable cash inflows which are largely               者。就減值評估而言，資產按
                  independent of the cash inflows from other assets or groups              獨立可識別現金流入的最低分
                  of assets (cash-generating units). Non-financial assets other            類組合（現金產生單位）分類，
                  than goodwill that suffered an impairment are reviewed                   其所產生的現金流入基本上獨
                  for possible reversal of the impairment at the end of each               立於其他資產或資產組別。出
                  reporting period.                                                        現減值的非金融資產（商譽除
                                                                                           外）於各報告期末檢討是否可
                                                                                           能撥回減值。

     2.13 Offsetting financial instruments                                         2.13 抵銷金融工具

          Financial assets and liabilities are offset and the net amount              當本集團現有法定可強制執行

          and there is an intention to settle on a net basis or realise the           現資產及結算負債時，有關金
          asset and settle the liability simultaneously. The Group has                融資產與負債可互相抵銷，並
          also entered into arrangements that do not meet the criteria                在資產負債表報告其淨值。本
          for offsetting but still allow for the related amounts to be                集團亦已訂立不符合抵銷標準

          termination of a contract.                                                  金額（如歸類為破產或中止合

                  proportion of variable and fixed overhead expenditure,                   接勞工以及適當比例的可變及
                  the latter being allocated on the basis of normal operating              固定經常開支（後者按一般運
                  capacity. Cost includes the reclassification from equity of              作產能分配）。成本包括自權
                  any gains or losses on qualifying cash flow hedges relating              益重新分類與購買原材料有關
                  to purchases of raw material but excludes borrowing costs.               的合資格現金流量對沖的任何
                  Costs are assigned to individual items of inventory on                   收益或虧損，但不包括借款成
                  the basis of weighted average costs. Costs of purchased                  本。成本按加權平均成本分配
                  inventory are determined after deducting rebates and                     至個別存貨項目。購買存貨的
                  discounts. Net realisable value is the estimated selling price           成本乃經扣減回扣及折讓後釐
                  in the ordinary course of business less the estimated costs              定。可變現淨值按日常業務過
                  of completion and the estimated costs necessary to make                  程中的估計售價減完成估計成

                                                                                           算。

            2.15 Trade receivables                                                      2.15 貿易應收款項

                  Trade receivables are amounts due from customers for                     貿易應收款項為在日常業務過
                  goods sold or services performed in the ordinary course of               程中就銷售的商品或提供的服
                  business. Trade receivables are generally due for settlement             務而應收客戶的款項。貿易應
                  within 30-90 days and therefore are all classified as current.           收款項一般於 30 至 90 日內進
                                                                                           行結算，因此全部被分類為流
                                                                                           動資產。

                  Trade receivables are recognised initially at the amount                 當貿易應收款項按公平值確認
                  of consideration that is unconditional unless they contain               時，初步按無條件的代價金額
                  significant financing components, when they are recognised               確認，惟彼等包含重大融資部
                  at fair value. The Group holds the trade receivables with the            分除外。本集團持有貿易應收
                  objective to collect the contractual cash flows and therefore            款項旨在收回合約現金流量，

            2.19 Current and deferred income tax                                            2.19 即期及遞延所得稅

                  The income tax expense or credit for the period is the tax                  本期間的所得稅開支或抵免指
                  payable on the current period’s taxable income based on the                 就本期間應課稅收入按各司法
                  applicable income tax rate for each jurisdiction adjusted by                權區的適用所得稅稅率支付的
                  changes in deferred tax assets and liabilities attributable to              稅項（就暫時差額及未動用稅
                  temporary differences and to unused tax losses.                             項虧損應佔的遞延稅項資產及
                                                                                              負債變動作出調整）。
                  Current income tax
                                                                                              即期所得稅
                  The current income tax charge is calculated on the basis
                  of the tax laws enacted or substantively enacted at the                     即期所得稅支出根據本公司附
                  end of the reporting period in the countries where the                      屬公司營運及產生應課稅收入
                  Company ’ s subsidiaries operate and generate taxable                       所在的國家於報告期末已頒佈
                  income. Management periodically evaluates positions taken                   或實質已頒佈的稅法計算。管
                  in tax returns with respect to situations in which applicable               理層就適用稅務法規須作出詮
                  tax regulation is subject to interpretation. It establishes                 釋的情況定期評估報稅表的狀
                  provisions where appropriate on the basis of amounts                        況，並須在適用情況下根據預
                  expected to be paid to the tax authorities.                                 期向稅務機關支付的稅款計提
                                                                                              撥備。

                  Deferred income tax                                                         遞延所得稅

                  Deferred income tax is provided in full, using the liability                 遞延所得稅採用負債法就資產
                  method, on temporary differences arising between the tax                     及負債的稅基與其在綜合財務
                  bases of assets and liabilities and their carrying amounts in                報表中的賬面值之間的暫時差
                  the consolidated financial statements. However, deferred                     額計提全額撥備。然而，倘遞
                  tax liabilities are not recognised if they arise from the initial            延稅項負債於初步確認商譽時
                  recognition of goodwill. Deferred income tax is also not                     產生，則不予確認；倘自初步
                  accounted for if it arises from initial recognition of an asset              確認一項交易（業務合併除外）
                  or liability in a transaction other than a business combination              中的資產或負債而產生的遞延
                  that at the time of the transaction affects neither accounting               所得稅於交易時對會計或應課
                  nor taxable profit or loss. Deferred income tax is determined                稅損益並不構成影響，則該遞
                  using tax rates (and laws) that have been enacted or                         延所得稅亦不會列賬。遞延所
                  substantively enacted by the end of the reporting period and                 得稅乃使用於報告期末已頒佈
                  are expected to apply when the related deferred income tax                   或實質已頒佈且預期將於相關
                  asset is realised or the deferred income tax liability is settled.           遞延所得稅資產變現或遞延所
                                                                                               得稅負債結算時適用的稅率
                                                                                              （及稅法）釐定。

                  The deferred tax liability in relation to investment property               按公平值計量的投資物業有關
                  that is measured at fair value is determined assuming the                   的遞延稅項負債乃假設物業將
                  property will be recovered entirely through sale.                           透過出售全部收回而釐定。

          that future taxable amounts will be available to utilise those             來應課稅款項可動用該等暫時
          temporary differences and losses.                                          差額及虧損的情況下予以確
                                                                                     認。

          Deferred tax liabilities and assets are not recognised for                 倘本公司能夠控制撥回暫時差
          temporary differences between the carrying amount and                      額的時間，且該等差額可能不
          tax bases of investments in foreign operations where the                   會於可見將來撥回，則不會就
          company is able to control the timing of the reversal of the               海外業務的投資賬面值與稅基
          temporary differences and it is probable that the differences              之間的暫時差額確認遞延稅項
          will not reverse in the foreseeable future.                                負債及資產。

          Deferred tax assets and liabilities are offset when there is               當存在合法可強制執行的權利
          a legally enforceable right to offset current tax assets and               將即期稅項資產與負債抵銷，
          liabilities and when the deferred tax balances relate to the               且遞延稅項結餘涉及同一稅務
          same taxation authority. Current tax assets and tax liabilities            機關，遞延稅項資產與負債互
          are offset where the entity has a legally enforceable right to             相抵銷。倘實體擁有合法可強
          offset and intends either to settle on a net basis, or to realise          制執行的權利抵銷並有意以淨
          the asset and settle the liability simultaneously.                         額結算或變現資產及同時償付
                                                                                     負債，則即期稅項資產與稅項
                                                                                     負債互相抵銷。

          Current and deferred tax is recognised in profit or loss,                  除與於其他全面收入或直接於
          except to the extent that it relates to items recognised in                權益確認的項目有關外，即期
          other comprehensive income or directly in equity. In this                  及遞延稅項於損益內確認。於
          case, the tax is also recognised in other comprehensive                    此情況下，稅項亦分別於其他
          income or directly in equity, respectively.                                全面收入或直接於權益確認。

                       The Group applies the IFRS 9 simplified approach                           本集團採用國際財
                       to measuring expected credit losses which uses                             務報告準則第9號

                       receivables.                                                               之簡化方法計量預
                                                                                                  期信用損失，為所

                                                                                                  期虧損。
                       To measure the expected credit losses, trade
                                                                                                  為計算預期信用損
                       receivables have been grouped based on shared
                       credit risk characteristics and aging analysis. The                        失，已根據共有信
                       expected loss rates are based on the payment                               用風險特徵及賬齡
                       profiles of sales over a period of 12 months before                        分析將貿易應收款
                       31 December 2018 or 1 January 2018 respectively                            項分組。預期虧損
                       and the corresponding historical credit losses
                       experienced within this period. The historical loss                        率乃分別基於 2018
                       rates are adjusted to reflect current and forward-                         年 12 月 31 日 或
                       looking information on macroeconomic factors                               2018 年 1 月 1 日 之
                       affecting the ability of the customers to settle the                       前 12 個月期間的銷
                       receivables.
                                                                                                  售付款情況及該期
                                                                                                  間內所錄得之相應
                                                                                                  過往信貸虧損。過
                                                                                                  往虧損率已經調整
                                                                                                  以反映影響客戶結
                                                                                                  清應收款項能力的
                                                                                                  宏觀經濟因素的現
                                                                                                  時及前瞻性資料。


                       On that basis, the loss allowance as at 31                                  按 該 基 準 計 算，
                       December 2018 and 1 January 2018 (on adoption                               2018 年 12 月 31 日

                                                                                                  （採納國際財務報告

                  (a)    Risk management                                                     (a)   風險管理

                         The Group’s objectives when managing capital are to                       本集團的資本管理目標
                                                                                                   為

                         •     safeguard the Group ’ s ability to continue as                •     保障本集團的持續運
                               a going concern, so that they can continue to                       營，以繼續為股東提供
                               provide returns for shareholders and benefits for                   回報及為其他股權持有
                               other stakeholders, and                                             人帶來利益，及

                         •     maintain an optimal capital structure to reduce               •     保持最優資本架構以降
                               the cost of capital.                                                低資本成本。

                         In order to maintain or adjust the capital structure, the                 為維持或調整資本架
                         Group may adjust the amount of dividends paid to                          構，本集團可調整分派
                         shareholders, or return capital to shareholders, issue                    予股東的股息金額，或
                         new shares or sell assets to reduce debt.                                 向股東返還資本、發行
                                                                                                   新股或出售資產以減少
                                                                                                   債務。

                         As the Group has no external borrowing in 2018 and                        由於本集團於 2018 年及
                         2017, the directors consider that the capital risk is low.                2017 年並無向外借款，
                                                                                                   董事認為資本風險較低。

      4.    CRITICAL ESTIMATES, JUDGEMENTS AND ERRORS                             4.   重大估計、判斷及失誤
            The preparation of financial statements requires the use of                編製財務報表需要使用會計估計，
            accounting estimates which, by definition, will seldom equal the           顧名思義，該等估計甚少符合實際
            actual results. Management also needs to exercise judgement in             結果。管理層亦須在應用本集團會
            applying the Group’s accounting policies.                                  計政策時作出判斷。

            Estimates and judgements are continually evaluated. They are               估計及判斷會予以持續評估，且基
            based on historical experience and other factors, including                於歷史經驗及其他因素，包括對未
            expectations of future events that may have a financial impact             來事件的預期，而該等事件可能對
            on the entity and that are believed to be reasonable under the             實體造成財務影響且據信在某些情
            circumstances.                                                             況下屬合理。

            4.1 Critical accounting estimates                                          4.1 重大會計估計

                  (a)    Net realisable value of inventories                             (a)   存貨的可變現淨值

                         Net realisable value of inventories is the estimated                  存貨的可變現淨值按日
                         selling price in the ordinary course of business, less                常業務過程中的估計售
                         estimated costs of completion and selling expenses.                   價減估計完成成本及銷
                         These estimates are based on the current market                       售開支計算。該等估計
                         condition and historical experience of manufacturing                  乃基於當前市況以及製
                         and selling products of similar nature. Management                    造及銷售類似性質產品
                         reassesses these estimates at each balance sheet                      的過往經驗作出。管理
                         date.                                                                 層於各資產負債表日期
                                                                                               重估該等估計。

      17. FINANCIAL ASSETS AT FAIR VALUE THROUGH PROFIT                            17. 按公平值計入損益的金融資產
            OR LOSS

            (i)   Classification of financial assets at fair value through           (i)     按公平值計入損益的金融資產
                  profit or loss                                                             的分類

                  The Group classifies the following financial assets at fair                本集團將下列金融資產分類為
                  value through profit or loss (FVPL):                                       按公平值計入損益（按公平值
                                                                                             計入損益）：

                  •      debt investments that do not qualify for measurement                •    不合資格按攤銷成本或

35. RELATED PARTY TRANSACTIONS                                          35. 關聯方交易

   Parties are considered to be related if one party has the ability,        如一方有能力直接或間接控制另一
   directly or indirectly, to control the other party or exercise            方或對另一方作出財務及經營決策
   significant influence over the other party in making financial            行使重大影響力，則各方被視為有
   and operation decisions. Parties are also considered to be                關聯。如彼等受共同控制，各方亦
   related if they are subject to common control. Members of key             被視為有關聯。本集團主要管理層
   management and their close family member of the Group are also            成員及其近親亦被視為關聯方。
   considered as related parties.

   Save as disclosed elsewhere in this consolidated financial                除本綜合財務資料其他部分所披露
   information, the Group has the following related transactions,            者外，本集團有以下關聯交易，該
   and they were carried out in the ordinary course of the Group’s           等交易於本集團日常業務過程中進
   business and were determined based on mutually agreed terms.              行及基於相互協定的條款釐定。

   Name and relationship with related parties:                               關聯方名稱及與關聯方的關係：
